*Wenxiao Wang,Priyatham Kattakinda,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有智能体优化方法的"一次性增益"问题，发现仅有RELAI-VCL方法能在持续学习场景中实现优化增益的复合累积，关键在于在优化循环中引入回归控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体优化方法的评估通常采用静态、单次优化设置，即针对固定基准测试优化一次后报告改进效果。然而，实际部署场景需要递归优化——随着新失败和新任务的出现不断优化。这引发了一个核心问题：优化驱动的增益是否能复合？即智能体经过一轮优化后，能否在新任务上再次优化而不损害第一轮获得的增益？

Method: 构建了两阶段持续学习评估框架，使用Terminal-Bench 2.0中的困难任务。比较了三种智能体套件优化方法（GEPA、Meta Harness和RELAI-VCL），在相同的优化预算下进行评估。RELAI-VCL（可验证持续学习）在优化循环中引入了回归控制机制。

Result: 在传统静态单阶段设置中，三种方法均优于基线智能体。但在引入新任务后，方法表现显著分化：GEPA的优化智能体迁移性能低于未优化基线；Meta Harness迁移良好但无法在第二轮优化中继续改进；仅RELAI-VCL能正向迁移到未见任务并在新任务纳入优化目标后持续改进。RELAI-VCL在所有评估阶段均达到最高通过率，终身平均通过率为76.4%（GEPA为66.0%，Meta Harness为64.6%，基线为58.7%）。

Conclusion: 优化增益仅在优化循环中内置回归控制时才能复合累积。回归控制提供了针对无法泛化的捷径解的归纳偏置，这是实现持续优化和增益累积的关键机制。

Abstract: Most reported gains from agent-optimization methods are one-shot: an agent is optimized against a fixed benchmark and the resulting improvement is reported as if it were a stable property of the method. This does not test the setting that matters for deployed agents, where optimization is applied recursively as new failures and new tasks appear over time. The central question this raises is whether optimizer-driven gains compound: after an agent has been optimized once, can it be optimized again on newly arrived tasks without eroding the gains the first round produced? We study this question with a two-phase continual-learning evaluation built from hard tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.0, comparing three approaches to agent-harness optimization (GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI's Verifiable Continual Learning, RELAI-VCL) under identical optimization budgets. All three methods improve over the baseline agent in the conventional, static, single-phase setting. However, once new tasks are introduced, the methods diverge sharply: GEPA's optimized agent transfers below the unoptimized baseline, Meta Harness transfers well but fails to improve further once given a second optimization budget, and RELAI-VCL is the only method that both transfers positively to unseen tasks and continues improving after those tasks are folded into the optimization objective, reaching the highest pass rate at every evaluated stage and the highest lifelong average pass rate overall (76.4% vs. 66.0% for GEPA, 64.6% for Meta Harness, and 58.7% for the baseline). Our key observation was that optimization gains compounded only when regression control was built into the optimization loop, providing an inductive bias against shortcut solutions that fail to generalize.

</details>


### [121] [AI-accelerated End-to-End Framework for Rapid Professional Upskilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14044)
*Tam Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Robert Ogburn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出端到端AI加速技能提升框架，覆盖知识获取、内容开发、审核、教学和评估五个阶段，并通过NASBA认证、NVIDIA认证考试通过率及大规模风险数据集生成验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预计到2030年59%的劳动力需要技能重塑或提升，而企业填补技能缺口的时间从2014年的约3天增至2018年的36天；现有框架仅加速单一阶段且缺乏行业验证，亟需端到端的加速方案。

Method: 构建端到端框架，将AI加速应用于知识获取、内容开发、内容审核与验证、教学以及评估开发五个阶段，重点兼顾生产效率与学习效率。

Result: 获美国州会计委员会协会（NASBA）审核批准并授予继续职业教育学分；3名学员在极短时间内通过NVIDIA Agentic AI认证考试，另有14名在训；知识库支持生成包含1267项风险条目的多智能体AI系统风险数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架在全流程加速技能提升方面可行且有效，为行业提供了经过外部权威验证的解决方案，具备实际生产与学习价值。

Abstract: By 2030, 59 of every 100 workers will need reskilling or upskilling, yet the average time to close an enterprise skills gap grew from roughly 3 days in 2014 to 36 days in 2018. Most current frameworks accelerate single stages of upskilling programs and generally lack industry validation. We present an end-to-end framework that applies AI acceleration across five stages of knowledge acquisition, content development, content review and verification, teaching, and assessment development; with a strong focus on both production and learning efficiency. Three strong external signals validates the framework: the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy reviewed and approved an upskilling program built on the framework for continuing-professional-education credits; 3 learners followed the program and passed the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in a significantly short amount of time, with 14 more in progress; the program's knowledge base supports complex downstream analysis such as the production of a robust 1,267 risk item dataset for managing multi-agent AI system risks.

</details>


### [122] [Earthquaker-AI: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework with Rubric-Based Assessment for Primary School Earthquake Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14046)
*Xanthi Kokkinou,Chaido Mizeli,Nafsika Koulaxidou,Marina Delianidi,Konstantinos Diamantaras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Earthquaker-AI，一个结合教育机器人与检索增强生成(RAG)对话AI助手的混合教育框架，旨在提升小学生的地震应急准备和应对能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是在原有获奖STEM项目Earthquaker的基础上，从单纯的机械模拟扩展到认知和元认知处理层面，以更好地培养小学生的地震安全意识和应急反应能力，促进技术素养、自我调节能力和负责任的数字系统使用。

Method: 该方法整合了Lego WeDo2机器人组件和RAG对话AI助手。机器人通过传感器和执行器模拟地震响应，让学生与防护行动的有形表示进行交互。AI助手作为引导学习机制，将学生回答与安全指南对齐，提供基于量表的口头反馈。系统采用渐进式学习路径：低年级使用二维量表评估选择题；中年级使用三轴量表评估动作序列选择；高年级要求书面回答并使用四维量表（包括表达清晰度）评估。对话模块通过RAG技术将学生查询与官方指南进行语义匹配。

Result: 实验评估显示系统具有高度的基础性和准确性，幻觉率低。系统成功实现了动手参与、信息处理和反思实践的有机结合，有效支持不同年级学生的认知发展需求。

Conclusion: Earthquaker-AI通过结合机器人技术、评估量表和人工智能，成功促进了学生的技术素养、自我调节能力和危机管理技能的早期培养，为小学地震安全教育提供了一个有效的综合解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents Earthquaker-AI, a hybrid educational framework building upon a previously implemented educational robotics project by integrating a conversational AI assistant based on Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It aims to enhance earthquake preparedness and conscious action among primary-school students. The system extends the award-winning STEM project Earthquaker moving from mechanical simulation with Lego WeDo2 to cognitive and metacognitive processing. The robotics component uses Lego WeDo2 automation to simulate seismic response, letting students interact with sensors and actuators as tangible representations of protective actions. The assistant operates as a guided learning mechanism aligning student responses with safety guidelines, while providing rubric-based verbal feedback that supports self-regulated learning and calmness under emergency conditions. Earthquaker-AI follows a progressive learning trajectory aligned with cognitive development. In early grades, the focus is on basic recognition of safety actions through multiple-choice questions, assessed via a two-dimensional rubric. In middle grades, students identify correct action sequences through multiple-choice questions, evaluated via a three-axis rubric. In upper grades, the approach shifts to verbal production, requiring short written responses assessed via a four-dimensional rubric that includes clarity of expression. The dialogic module uses RAG to match student queries semantically with official guidelines, generating safe, accurate responses. Experimental evaluation shows high groundedness and accuracy, with a low hallucination rate. Overall, Earthquaker-AI combines hands-on engagement, information processing, and reflective practice. Combining robotics, rubrics, and AI promotes technological literacy, self-regulation, and responsible use of digital systems, contributing to early crisis-management skills.

</details>


### [123] [Deep Interaction: An Efficient Human-AI Interaction Method for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14049)
*Hefeng Zhou,Jinxuan Zhang,Jiong Lou,Yuxin Liu,Chaochao Lu,Jingjing Qu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Deep Interaction机制，通过直接编辑原始回答并提炼为蒸馏提示来纠正CoT推理错误，在STEM任务上纠正成功率提升超25%，token使用减少约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的CoT推理错误纠正方式效率低下：重新生成可能重复犯错，用户标记错误步骤后仍可能再次出错，缺乏精确干预机制。

Method: 提出Deep Interaction机制：1)允许直接编辑原始回答，保留正确推理步骤的同时纠正错误部分；2)将编辑后的CoT提炼为蒸馏提示；3)用该提示引导LLM沿修正后的推理路径生成回答。

Result: 在STEM推理任务上，纠正成功率提升超过25%，token使用量减少约40%，相比基线方法显著提高效率和效果。

Conclusion: Deep Interaction提供了一种高效的人机交互机制，能够精确纠正LLM的CoT推理错误，在提升纠正效果的同时大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, when errors occur, current interaction approaches typically involve re-generating another response that may make mistakes again, or users laboriously flag the faulty step in follow-up turns that may get responses <You are right, I made a mistake here> followed by similar errors recurring. To address this issue, we propose an efficient human intervention mechanism for precisely correcting reasoning errors in LLMs, termed Deep Interaction. Our approach enables direct editing of the original response, allowing erroneous parts to be corrected while preserving accurate reasoning steps. We refine the edited CoT into a distilled prompt, which then steers the LLM along the corrected reasoning path. Experimental results show that our method achieves over a 25% improvement in correction success rate and reduces token usage by approximately 40% on STEM tasks reasoning compared to baseline approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Learning Latency-Aware Orchestration for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13359)
*Xi Shi,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出LAMaS框架，通过关键路径感知的信用分配和运行时轻量级控制器，在保持准确性的同时将多智能体系统端到端延迟降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS编排方法主要优化任务性能和推理成本，但延迟问题未被充分解决。端到端延迟由关键执行路径决定，仅减少总成本无法可靠降低延迟。此外，在保持准确性的同时优化延迟具有挑战性：朴素的延迟优化可能错误分配算子级信用并降低任务准确性。

Method: 提出LAMaS延迟感知编排框架，包含两个层面：(1)训练时通过关键路径感知信用分配的约束优化学习延迟感知执行图；(2)推理时配合轻量级控制器，根据运行时证据自适应消除冗余的未来智能体交互。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，LAMaS在基于学习的MAS基线中实现最佳延迟，端到端延迟降低超过50%，同时保持竞争力或更好的准确性。框架具有模块化特性，可最小改动迁移到其他MAS并持续产生延迟降低效果。

Conclusion: LAMaS成功解决了MAS中延迟优化与准确性保持的权衡问题，通过关键路径感知的训练时优化和运行时自适应控制，实现了显著的延迟降低，为多智能体系统的高效编排提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) coordinate multiple LLM-powered agents through structured workflows, gaining reasoning power but incurring high inference latency from multi-step execution and repeated model invocations. Existing orchestration methods primarily optimize task performance and inference cost, leaving latency largely unaddressed. In MAS, end-to-end latency is governed by the critical execution path, so reducing total cost alone does not reliably reduce latency. Moreover, optimizing latency while preserving accuracy remains non-trivial: naive latency optimization can misassign operator-level credit and degrade task accuracy. To address this gap, we propose Latency-Aware Multi-agent System (LAMaS), a latency-aware orchestration framework for learning-based multi-agent systems. LAMaS addresses this challenge at two levels: at training time, it learns latency-aware execution graphs through constrained optimization with critical-path-aware credit assignment; at inference time, since a graph committed at training time cannot exploit runtime evidence, it complements graph construction with a lightweight controller that adaptively eliminates redundant future agent interactions as execution unfolds. Experiments on four benchmarks show that LAMaS achieves the best latency among evaluated learning-based MAS baselines, reducing end-to-end latency by over 50\% while maintaining competitive or better accuracy. LAMaS is also modular and transfers to other MAS with minimal changes, consistently yielding latency reductions.

</details>


### [125] [Equilibrium stability as a driver of cooperation among Q-learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13607)
*Janusz M. Meylahn,Maximilian Schäfer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在持续探索条件下定价算法的学习动态，发现合作策略可以在时间平均意义上占主导地位，并推导出了预测合作策略何时占主导的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注强化学习算法收敛到合作策略的概率，通常假设探索随时间消失。然而实际部署的算法为了保持对变化环境的适应性，往往会持续进行探索。这一现实观察促使作者研究持续探索条件下的学习动态，此时关注点从算法是否收敛到特定策略转变为算法在多大比例时间内采取合作策略。

Method: 以具有单期记忆的重复囚徒困境为基准案例，研究持续探索下的高维随机学习动态。通过分析Q学习过程的期望动态，推导出一个预测合作策略在时间平均意义上何时占主导地位的边界条件，并采用epsilon-greedy Q学习算法进行大量仿真验证。

Result: 在时间平均意义上，合作策略可以占主导地位，即使算法不会收敛到特定策略。推导出的边界条件能够有效预测epsilon-greedy Q学习算法下非背叛主导行为的出现。大量仿真实验验证了该边界条件的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 在持续探索的现实设定下，定价算法可能表现出以时间平均为基础的合作行为主导现象，而非传统研究中关注的收敛性质。这一发现对理解实际市场中算法合谋的动态特征和反垄断政策制定具有重要意义。

Abstract: Algorithmic collusion among pricing algorithms has raised concerns about sustained supra-competitive prices and their implications for social welfare. Existing work has largely focused on the probability that reinforcement-learning algorithms converge to cooperative strategies, typically under the assumption that exploration vanishes over time. Motivated by the observation that algorithms deployed in practice are likely to continue exploring in order to remain adaptive to changing environments, we study learning dynamics under constant exploration. In this setting, the relevant question is no longer whether an algorithm converges to a particular strategy profile, but rather what fraction of time the algorithms spend playing cooperative strategies. Even in the benchmark case of the repeated Prisoner's Dilemma with one-period memory, this yields high-dimensional stochastic learning dynamics, for which a complete analytic treatment is intractable. We show that cooperative strategies can be dominant in this time-averaged sense and derive a boundary predicting when such dominance arises, based on the expected dynamics of the Q-learning process. Extensive simulations show that this boundary is a strong predictor for non-defection-dominated behaviour under epsilon-greedy Q-learning.

</details>


### [126] [Extending Liquid Rank Toward Multi-Source Reputation Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13615)
*Nejc Znidar,Anton Kolonin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了液体排名声誉系统，提出了一个能够将多个异构声誉源聚合并融合为统一声誉分数的框架，支持内外部声誉信号的整合与权重控制，适用于复杂社会技术环境中的声誉治理机制设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液体排名声誉系统缺乏对多源异构声誉数据的统一聚合能力，无法有效融合内外部声誉信号，难以适应涉及人类与机器代理的多样化治理与协调场景，因此需要一种灵活可控的多源声誉融合机制。

Method: 提出了一种扩展的液体排名声誉系统框架，引入显式权重与融合机制，支持将内部生成的声誉与外部声誉信号（如跨上下文和子系统的参与贡献）进行加权聚合，形成统一的声誉评分，并提供细粒度的相对影响控制。

Result: 该框架成功实现了多源异构声誉的统一聚合与融合，能够灵活调整各声誉源的权重与相对影响力，增强了系统对不同治理与协调场景的适应性，为复杂社会技术环境中的声誉治理提供了基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅扩展了现有液体排名系统的能力，还为复杂社会技术环境中设计声誉驱动的治理机制提供了灵活、可控的基础，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an extension of liquid rank reputation systems that enables the aggregation and blending of multiple heterogeneous reputation sources into a unified reputation score. The proposed framework supports the incorporation of external reputational signals alongside internally generated reputation, allowing influence to reflect participation and contribution across multiple contexts and subsystems. By introducing explicit weighting and blending mechanisms, the model provides fine-grained control over the relative impact of individual reputation sources, making it adaptable to diverse governance and coordination scenarios involving both human and machine agents. The resulting approach extends existing liquid rank systems and offers a flexible foundation for designing reputation-based governance mechanisms in complex socio-technical environments.

</details>


### [127] [Social Simulations: from Agent-Based Modeling to Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13693)
*Erica Cau,Andrea Failla,Valentina Pansanella,Giulio Rossetti*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本章综述了社会模拟从经典基于代理模型到大型语言模型增强模拟，最终发展为社会数字孪生的演进过程，探讨了各范式的方法论基础、应用、优势与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理社会模拟领域从抽象模型向现实计算表征的演进轨迹，明确各发展阶段的方法论基础、应用价值与技术边界，为研究者提供该领域的全景式认知框架。

Method: 采用历史演进视角，对比分析三种范式：（1）经典基于代理模型（显式行为规则）；（2）大型语言模型增强的AI模拟；（3）社会数字孪生（高保真、数据驱动的现实社会技术系统表征）。逐一论述各范式的方法论基础、应用场景、优势与局限性。

Result: 清晰呈现了社会模拟领域的范式演进路径：从探索一般社会机制的抽象模型，逐步转向特定社会系统的高保真计算表征。明确了各阶段的特征：经典ABM具有显式规则可解释性，LLM增强模拟提升了行为生成能力，社会数字孪生实现了现实系统的高 fidelity 映射。总结了各范式的适用边界与方法论挑战。

Conclusion: 社会模拟正经历从机制探索导向向系统表征导向的根本性转变，技术融合推动了现实社会技术系统建模能力的跃升，但同时也带来了数据依赖、计算复杂度与可解释性等新的方法论挑战。

Abstract: This book chapter covers the evolution of social simulation from classical agent-based models, in which agents interact according to explicitly defined behavioral rules, to AI-enhanced simulations based on Large Language Models and, ultimately, Social Digital Twins: high-fidelity, data-driven representations of real-world socio-technical systems. Along this trajectory, we discuss the main methodological foundations, applications, advantages, and limitations of each paradigm, highlighting the progressive shift from abstract models designed to investigate general social mechanisms toward increasingly realistic computational representations of specific social systems.

</details>


### [128] [Pezego-HITL: A policy-grounded large language model architecture for agricultural extension in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13934)
*Shunbao Li,Zhipeng Yuan,Amoako Ofori,Benedicta Y. Fosu-Mensah,Yang Li,Manu Kenchappa Junjanna,Qing Xue,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了P-EVAL框架，通过策略约束的评估方法将大语言模型应用于小农户农业决策支持系统，在加纳实地测试中实现了政策对齐率0.94、农艺效用率0.95，并将延迟降低55%，为可信AI农业推广提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在农业决策支持中的应用日益增加，但在小农户高风险作物保护场景中，仅依靠输出质量基准评估不足。需要建立统一的评估框架来联合考量安全合规性、有用性、操作延迟和专家监督工作量等关键因素。

Method: 提出P-EVAL（策略导向专家校准验证协议）框架，将策略约束的大语言模型评估形式化为自适应计算分配问题。采用记忆路由架构（Pezego-HITL）进行结构化检索增强生成，并在模拟查询数据库（1,240个案例）上进行评估。通过离线judge校准与金标准人类专家决策对齐（κ=0.77），并使用开源模型验证泛化性。

Result: 记忆路由架构将政策对齐率（PAR）提升至0.94，农艺效用率（AUR）达0.95；通过59.6%的缓存复用率，P95延迟降低55%（从28.6s降至12.9s）。使用Qwen3.5-9B-DeepSeek-V4-Flash模型达到PAR 0.86和54.5%延迟降低（至10.2s）。向30名加纳推广服务官员和36名小农户进行的问卷调研验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了策略导向的结构化检索增强生成与验证记忆路由方法能够显式处理安全-效用-延迟权衡，为小农户农业系统中可信赖的AI驱动推广服务提供了可扩展模板。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in agricultural decision-support settings, yet high-stakes crop protection in smallholder agriculture requires more than output-quality benchmarks. Over a two-year design and evaluation programme, we formalise policy-constrained large language model assessment as an adaptive compute allocation problem that jointly captures safety compliance, helpfulness, operational latency, and expert supervision workload. We introduce P-EVAL (Policy-grounded Expert-calibrated VALidation protocol), a unified evaluation framework for policy-grounded decision support, evaluating the architecture on a simulated field query database consisting of 1,240 cases. The protocol is instantiated on the Pezego advisory architecture (Pezego-HITL) and evaluated in Ghana. Following offline judge calibration against gold-standard human expert decisions ($κ= 0.77$), we evaluate the architectural performance under simulated query workloads. Under P-EVAL, our memory-routed architecture improves the Policy Alignment Rate (PAR) to 0.94 and the Agronomic Utility Rate (AUR) to 0.95, while reducing P95 latency by 55% (from 28.6s to 12.9s) through a 59.6% cache reuse ratio. We also demonstrate generalisability using the open-source \texttt{Qwen3.5-9B-DeepSeek-V4-Flash} model, achieving a PAR of 0.86 and a 54.5% latency reduction (to 10.2s). To evaluate practical utility and socio-technical integration, we administer detailed questionnaires to Ghanaian Extension Services Officers ($N=30$) and smallholder farmers ($N=36$). Taken together, this work demonstrates how policy-grounded structured retrieval-augmented generation with validated-memory routing makes safety-utility-latency trade-offs explicit, offering a scalable template for trustworthy AI-driven extension in smallholder farming systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Fair on the Surface: Transaction-Ordering Bias and MEV in Mysticeti DAG-based BFT Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13378)
*Iliya Mirzaei,Mohammad Javad Amiri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Mysticeti协议（Sui区块链的共识层）中存在的交易排序公平性问题，提出了三种可被利用的攻击方式，并给出了简单有效的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化应用中，交易排序具有实际的经济价值，攻击者可通过操纵交易顺序获取最大可提取价值（MEV）。Mysticeti作为Sui区块链的生产级共识协议，被认为能通过并行提议和Gas价格重排序实现公平排序，但这一假设需要验证。

Method: 通过分析Mysticeti协议的DAG排序机制，识别出三个层面的公平性漏洞：（1）同轮次区块按验证者索引排序导致的永久性优势；（2）Gas价格重排序使用稳定排序导致等费用交易保留原有偏序；（3）验证者通过选择性静默放大排序优势。在13个验证者的测试网络上量化评估这些漏洞的影响。

Result: 实验表明：（1）无攻击者情况下，低索引验证者在同轮次排序中约89%的时间获胜；（2）等Gas费用交易保留了原有偏序，使攻击者无需支付额外费用即可获利；（3）验证者通过选择性静默可将排序胜率提升至94%以上。同时验证了Mysticeti在标准拜占庭故障阈值下仍保持容错性。

Conclusion: Mysticeti的交易排序公平性假设在实践中被打破，但这些漏洞不影响其拜占庭容错安全性。提出了简单修复方案：用不可预测的随机密钥替代验证者索引作为同轮次排序的决胜机制。

Abstract: Distributed systems deployed in untrustworthy environments agree on a common transaction order through Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, and that order has real financial value in many decentralized applications: whoever influences it can profit at other users' expense, a problem known as maximal extractable value (MEV). Mysticeti is a state-of-the-art DAG-based BFT protocol in which many validators propose blocks in parallel, and the total order is derived from the resulting DAG afterward. Mysticeti is the consensus protocol powering Sui, a production blockchain with a market capitalization of roughly $3 billion, and it is widely believed to order transactions fairly, since many validators propose blocks in parallel and committed transactions are re-sorted by gas price before execution. We show this fairness assumption breaks down in practice, and the effect is already present on Sui's live network. First, when vertices of the committed graph are merged into a single total order, blocks from the same round are sorted by validator index, giving lower-indexed validators a permanent head start. In our evaluation on a 13-validator network with no attacker, the lower-indexed side wins same-round ordering about 89% of the time. Second, the gas-price re-sort intended to remove this bias uses a stable sort, so transactions paying equal fees (common at the reference gas price) retain the original biased order, letting an attacker profit without paying extra. Third, a validator can amplify this advantage by choosing when to stay silent, a fully legitimate action that violates no protocol rule; this raises its ordering win rate above 94%. We measure all three exploitations, verify that Mysticeti otherwise remains resilient below the standard Byzantine fault threshold, and propose a simple fix: replace the validator-index tiebreaker with an unpredictable, per-commit random key.

</details>


### [130] [gDMC: A Generic Distributed Model Counting Framework via Work-Stealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13634)
*Zhenghang Xu,Minghao Yin,Jumping Zhou,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一个通用的分布式精确模型计数框架，通过C++模板实现并行编排与求解逻辑解耦，采用自适应工作窃取策略，在竞赛基准测试中实现近线性加速比并显著优于现有分布式求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命题模型计数在概率推理中至关重要，但单核面临可扩展性瓶颈。现有分布式方法存在高初始化开销（静态分解）或架构僵化等问题，亟需一种高效灵活的分布式解决方案。

Method: 提出通用分布式框架，利用C++模板技术将并行编排与求解逻辑解耦，使现有优秀求解器能以最小修改实现并行化；引入自适应工作窃取策略确保有效负载均衡。

Result: 在竞赛基准测试上实现近线性扩展性，性能显著优于现有分布式求解器。

Conclusion: 该框架为分布式精确模型计数提供了高效、通用的解决方案，验证了解耦架构与自适应负载平衡的有效性。

Abstract: Propositional Model Counting ($\#\mathsf{SAT}$) is essential for probabilistic reasoning but faces scalability limits on single cores. Existing distributed approaches struggle with high initialization overheads (static decomposition) or rigid architecture. We propose a novel, generic framework for distributed \emph{exact} model counting. Leveraging C++ templates, our architecture decouples parallel orchestration from solving logic, enabling state-of-the-art solvers to be parallelized with minimal modification. We implement an adaptive work-stealing strategy that ensures effective load balancing. Experiments on competition benchmarks show that our approach achieves near-linear scalability and significantly outperforms existing distributed solvers.

</details>


### [131] [A Distributed Framework for Compiling and Reasoning with d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13642)
*Zhenghang Xu,Minghao Yin,jianan Wang,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个分布式知识编译器dkc，利用Cube-and-Conquer策略解决了大规模知识编译的计算瓶颈问题，并配套分布式推理引擎dreasoner支持高效的查询操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编译（KC）通过将命题公式转换为可处理的目标语言（如d-DNNF）实现高效推理。然而，随着现实问题复杂度的增加，离线编译阶段成为显著的计算瓶颈，常超出单节点系统的内存和时间限制。虽然分布式计算已成功应用于模型计数（#SAT），但由于难以在分布式节点间共享部分电路片段，将其扩展到知识编译仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出dkc——首个面向大规模Decision-DNNF生成的分布式知识编译器。采用Cube-and-Conquer策略将搜索空间划分为独立子问题，有效缓解了电路任务中工作窃取架构通常伴随的通信开销。同时提出dreasoner分布式推理引擎，能够在分布式d-DNNF结构上执行核心推理任务（包括模型计数、直接访问和均匀采样），即使在变量条件下也能工作。

Result: 基准测试实验表明，该分布式架构具有良好的扩展性，能够编译和查询超出当前最先进顺序编译器能力范围的复杂公式。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将分布式计算技术扩展至知识编译领域，突破了现有单节点系统在处理复杂公式时的局限性，为大规模知识编译和推理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Compilation (KC) is a powerful paradigm that enables efficient reasoning by transforming propositional formulas into tractable target languages, such as Deterministic, Decomposable Negation Normal Form (d-DNNF). However, as real-world problem instances grow in complexity, the offline compilation phase becomes a significant computational bottleneck, often exceeding the memory and temporal limits of single-node systems. While distributed computing has been successfully applied to model counting ($\#\mathsf{SAT}$), extending these techniques to knowledge compilation remains a challenge due to the difficulty of sharing partial circuit fragments across distributed nodes.
  In this paper, we propose dkc, the first distributed knowledge compiler designed for large-scale Decision-DNNF generation. Leveraging a Cube-and-Conquer strategy, dkc effectively partitions the search space into independent subproblems, mitigating the communication overhead typically associated with work-stealing architectures in circuit-based tasks. Recognizing that the utility of compilation lies in subsequent querying, we further introduce dreasoner, a distributed reasoning engine. dreasoner is capable of performing core inference tasks (including model counting, direct access, and uniform sampling) across a distributed d-DNNF structure, even under variable conditioning. Our experimental evaluation on benchmarks demonstrates that our distributed architecture scales effectively, enabling the compilation and querying of complex formulas that remain beyond the reach of state-of-the-art sequential compilers.

</details>


### [132] [Local Certification of Vertex and Edge Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13677)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Yi-Xuan Lee,Meng-Tsung Tsai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了图连通性的局部认证问题，为k-点连通性和k-边连通性建立了认证方案。对于k-边连通性，获得了O_k(log n)比特的上界和匹配的下界；对于k-点连通性，在Itai-Zehavi猜想下获得了Õ_k(√n)比特的方案；并证明了在特定稀疏图类上可以突破对数障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部认证是一种仅使用局部信息验证全局图性质的框架，先前工作已为2-点连通性、2-边连通性和3-点连通性建立了O(log n)比特的认证方案。本文旨在将研究扩展到一般的k-点连通性和k-边连通性，探索更广泛连通性性质的认证复杂度。

Method: 利用连通性与组合结构之间的联系，包括分支结构、欧拉子图和独立生成树等。开发了新方法来利用这些组合结构为连通性设计认证方案，并建立相应的下界证明。

Result: 对于k-边连通性，获得了O_k(log n)比特认证方案和匹配的Ω_k(log n)比特下界（k≥3）。对于k-点连通性，在Itai-Zehavi猜想下获得Õ_k(√n)比特方案。在有界扩张图上，2-边连通性允许常数大小证书；在有界度图上，2-点连通性允许常数大小证书。在一般图上，证明了2-点连通性的Ω(log(log* n))比特下界。

Conclusion: 本文显著推进了图连通性局部认证的研究边界，为k-边连通性建立了紧的界，为k-点连通性提供了多项式改进，并展示了在某些稀疏图类上可以突破对数障碍，同时证明了2-点连通性在一般图上的超常数下界。

Abstract: Local certification is a framework for verifying global graph properties using only local information. In this model, a prover assigns short labels, called certificates, to the vertices of a graph. Each vertex then exchanges certificates with its neighbors and performs a purely local check to determine whether the graph satisfies the desired property. This line of research has led to efficient certification schemes for a broad range of graph classes, including minor-closed families, topological graph classes, and graphs defined by forbidden subgraphs.
  In this paper, we study the local certification of graph connectivity. Prior work by Bousquet, Feuilloley, and Pierron (JPDC 2024) showed that $2$-vertex-connectivity, $2$-edge-connectivity, and $3$-vertex-connectivity admit $O(\log n)$-bit certificates, leveraging structural characterizations such as ear decompositions. We go substantially beyond these cases and investigate general $k$-vertex-connectivity and $k$-edge-connectivity. We develop new approaches that exploit connections between connectivity and combinatorial structures, including branchings, Eulerian subgraphs, and independent spanning trees.
  For $k$-edge-connectivity, we obtain an $O_k(\log n)$-bit certification scheme and prove a matching $Ω_k(\log n)$ lower bound for every $k\ge 3$. The lower bound also applies to $k$-vertex-connectivity. For $k$-vertex-connectivity, we obtain $\tilde{O}_k(\sqrt{n})$-bit certificates for every $k$ under a conjecture of Itai and Zehavi. We further show that, for $k=2$, the logarithmic barrier can be broken on sparse graph classes: $2$-edge-connectivity admits constant-size certificates in bounded-expansion graphs, and $2$-vertex-connectivity admits constant-size certificates in bounded-degree graphs. In contrast, for $2$-vertex-connectivity in general graphs, we prove an $Ω(\log(\log^\ast n))$-bit lower bound.

</details>
